                )
                ( Value /parts -> /Data/List/Cons[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                    ( /head=∀/Data/String
                    , /tail=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                    ) = /LLVM/llvmReversed
                    ( Value /lines -> /Data/List/Cons
                        ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                        , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                            [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                            ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                            , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                )
                            )
                        ) = /Data/List/Cons
                        ( Value /head ->
                            ( /Data/String/Text
                                ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                            ) = CStr
                            { csValue = "c" }
                        )
                        ( Value /tail -> /Data/List/Cons
                            [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                            ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                            , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                )
                            ) = /Data/List/Cons
                            ( Value /head ->
                                ( /Data/String/Text
                                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                ) = CStr
                                { csValue = "b" }
                            )
                            ( Value /tail -> /Data/List/Cons
                                [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                ( Value /head ->
                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                    ) = CStr
                                    { csValue = "a" }
                                )( Value /tail -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil )
                            )
                        )
                    )( Value /acc -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil )
                )
            )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "a,b,c" }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( Value /text ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "## Writing a module
                The parts of a module, each written as the text it is in a `.ll` file." }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( Value /p -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/llvmModule
                ( Value /functions -> /Data/List/Cons
                    ( /head=∀/Data/String
                    , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                    ) = /Data/List/Cons
                    ( Value /head -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/llvmDefine
                        ( Value /ret ->
                            ( /Data/String/Text
                                ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                            ) = CStr
                            { csValue = "i32" }
                        )
                        ( Value /name ->
                            ( /Data/String/Text
                                ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                            ) = CStr
                            { csValue = "main" }
                        )
                        ( Value /params ->
                            ( /Data/String/Text
                                ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                            ) = CStr
                            { csValue = "" }
                        )
                        ( Value /body -> /Data/List/Cons
                            [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                            ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                            , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                )
                            ) = /Data/List/Cons
                            ( Value /head ->
                                ( /Data/String/Text
                                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                ) = CStr
                                { csValue = "entry:" }
                            )
                            ( Value /tail -> /Data/List/Cons
                                [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                ( Value /head ->
                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                    ) = CStr
                                    { csValue = "  ret i32 0" }
                                )( Value /tail -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil )
                            )
                        )
                    )( Value /tail -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil )
                )( Value /externs -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil )
            )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                define i32 @main() {
                entry:
                  ret i32 0
                }
                " }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( Value /text ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "## Compiling functions
                What [llvm] compiles a function to, read as the text of the module it writes ([llvmText])." }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( Value /p -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/Example/llvmText( Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/llvmExArith = Value /LLVM/Example/llvmExArith ) )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                define i64 @main() {
                entry:
                  %r0 = mul i64 2, 3
                  %r1 = add i64 %r0, %x
                  ret i64 %r1
                }
                " }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( Value /p -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/Example/llvmText( Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/llvmExFloats = Value /LLVM/Example/llvmExFloats ) )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                define double @main() {
                entry:
                  %r0 = fmul double 1.5, 2.0
                  %r1 = fsub double %r0, 0.5
                  ret double %r1
                }
                " }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( Value /p -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/Example/llvmText( Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/llvmExLess = Value /LLVM/Example/llvmExLess ) )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                define i1 @main() {
                entry:
                  %r0 = icmp slt i64 %x, 3
                  ret i1 %r0
                }
                " }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( Value /p -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/Example/llvmText( Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/llvmExGreet = Value /LLVM/Example/llvmExGreet ) )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                declare void @exit(i32)
                @.str0 = private unnamed_addr constant [6 x i8] c"hello\00"
                declare i32 @puts(ptr)

                define i32 @main() {
                entry:
                  call i32 @puts(ptr @.str0)
                  %r0 = trunc i64 3 to i32
                  call void @exit(i32 %r0)
                  ret i32 0
                }
                " }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( Value /p -> ∀/Data/String = /LLVM/Example/llvmText( Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/llvmExCalls = Value /LLVM/Example/llvmExCalls ) )
            ( Value /result ->
                ( /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                ) = CStr
                { csValue = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                define i64 @main() {
                entry:
                  %r0 = add i64 %x, 1
                  %r1 = call i64 @"/LLVM/Example/llvmExTwice"(i64 %r0)
                  %r2 = mul i64 %r1, 2
                  ret i64 %r2
                }

                define i64 @"/LLVM/Example/llvmExTwice"(i64 %n) {
                entry:
                  %r0 = add i64 %n, %n
                  ret i64 %r0
                }
                " }
            )
        ]
    }
